        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 8 still makes draft creation happen only through the 800ms autosave path, while the publish flow immediately calls `syncembeds(postid, ...)` and `publishpost(postid, ...)`. if an author types content and clicks publish before the first autosave has created the row, there is no guaranteed `postid` for the primary publish path, so the plan has a runtime hole in the initial create-and-publish flow. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the cover fetch path is internally inconsistent. step 8 stores the selected cover on `posts.cover_media_id`, but step 5 says `usepost` fetches the post row plus `post_media` and `post_assets` and then 'resolves cover from `post.cover_media_id`'; because the cover picker is separate from body embeds and the plan never says to also link the chosen cover into `post_media`, a cover that is not embedded in the body would need its own direct media fetch or it will not be renderable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the inline video plan does not fit the existing media pipeline. `mediauploader` only returns `file` objects, and the current upload flow writes a public supabase url into `cloudflare_thumbnail_url`; meanwhile every existing video surface (`src/pages/artdetail/index.tsx`, `src/pages/resourcedetail/index.tsx`, and `src/pages/resources/hlsplayer.tsx`) expects `cloudflare_playback_hls_url` for playable video, so uploaded videos would not render through `hlsplayer` as planned. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed rpc auth path is internally inconsistent. `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role`, but its body authorizes with `auth.uid()` and `public.is_admin()`. in the current repo, edge functions authenticate the bearer separately with `supabase.auth.getuser(accesstoken)` and then run db work as service role (`supabase/functions/cloudflare-stream-ingest/index.ts:43-72`), so this sql function would not automatically see the caller's `auth.uid()` unless the plan adds a second authenticated client or rewrites the guard. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the storage/versioning sequence still has a race. step 3 uploads files under `post-bundles/bundles/<post_id>/<next_version>/...` before calling `register_bundle_version(...)`, but step 1 also makes the rpc compute `version = max(version) + 1` itself. concurrent uploads can therefore diverge between storage prefix and db version, and any rpc failure after upload leaves orphaned files because storage writes happened before the transactional sql step. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared step 3.11 in `plan_v6.md` with the vendored `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/timelinerenderer.tsx:66-85`. the plan's scaled visual branch wraps a bare `<visualclip .../>` in `<absolutefill style={{transform: scale(...)}}>` but does not include a surrounding `<sequence from=... durationinframes=...>`. in the reference, that branch wraps `<visualclipsequence .../>`, which is where clip timing is applied, so implementing the plan literally would make scaled non-position-override clips mount from frame 0 for the full composition instead of honoring `clip.at` and `getclipdurationinframes(...)`. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed cache contract for the `cut` step is technically unsafe because it uses only `hype.timeline.json` as the sentinel even though `cut.py` writes `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, and `hype.metadata.json` as one unit (`tools/cut.py:763-769`). later planned steps consume the extra outputs as required inputs (`tools/render_remotion.py:62-99` requires `--assets`, and the bridge copies `hype.assets.json`), so a partial prior run or manual deletion of assets/metadata would still be treated as 'cached' and cause downstream breakage. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: flagged that `--print-sql` still defaults `--timeline-id` to `<name>` even though `public.timelines.id` is a uuid column, so the default emitted `update` is not runnable as written. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: flagged that byte-preserving `hype.assets.json` into `public.timelines` remains technically incomplete because reigh-app resolves asset `file` values as http urls or `timeline-assets` bucket keys, while `cut.py` writes local absolute paths. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] audio-playback: audio playback: the port plan applies sanitized playback rate in `visualclip.tsx` but omits `playbackrate` from the planned `audiotrack.tsx`, leaving per-clip speed only partially implemented. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bundle-registration-auth: bundle registration auth: `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role` but authorizes with `auth.uid()`/`public.is_admin()` internally, which does not match the repo's existing edge function pattern where service-role clients validate the bearer separately before doing db work. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bundle-versioning: bundle versioning: step 3 uploads extracted files into a `<next_version>` storage prefix before the database allocates a version, but step 1 also computes `version = max(version) + 1` inside the rpc; concurrent uploads can mismatch storage and db state, and failed rpcs leave orphaned bundle files. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] community-section: success criterion 'all header-offset js styling goes through headeroffsetpadding()' is broader than what step 3 actually does (two css-string exceptions remain). (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements: did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the actual migration at `/users/user_c042661f/documents/banodoco-workspace/supabase/migrations/20260325070000_create_posts.sql`. the repository already defines `posts`, `post_media`, and `post_assets`, with `posts.body`, `posts.slug`, `posts.status` allowing `'archived'`, and ownership/rls keyed through `members.auth_user_id`; the plan still treats that schema as unreadable and proposes a materially different surface (`body_md`, `author_user_id`, draft/published-only status), which is not the minimal extension the brief asked for.   "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/.megaplan/audit.md\n+## T1 Audit - 2026-04-23\n+\n+No contradictions against the gate evidence were found. The repo mechanics below are the binding facts for later tasks.\n+\n+### A. Import style / sibling imports\n+\n+- `head -20` confirms plain flat imports, with no `sys.path.insert(...)` or similar path mutation in the audited files.\n+- `tools/refine.py` imports siblings directly, e.g. `from cut import ...`.\n+- `tools/cut.py` imports siblings directly, e.g. `from timeline import ...`.\n+- `tools/scenes.py` and `tools/shots.py` do not mutate `sys.path`; they are simple CLI scripts.\n+- `rg -n \"sys\\\\.path\\\\.insert|sys\\\\.path\\\\.append|import sys\"` across `tools/refine.py`, `tools/cut.py`, `tools/scenes.py`, and `tools/shots.py` returned no matches.\n+- Later files should mirror this style with plain sibling imports such as `import enriched_arrangement` or `from enriched_arrangement import ...`.\n+\n+### B. `cut.py` metadata nesting / tool fingerprints\n+\n+- `rg -n \"sources\\\\[|sources\\\\.setdefault|_tool_fingerprints\" tools/cut.py` hit only:\n+  - `_tool_fingerprints` at line 503\n+  - `sources[primary_asset] = primary_source` at lines 564 and 959\n+  - `tool_versions` writes at lines 590 and 974\n+- There are no `sources.setdefault(...)` hits.\n+- This confirms `sources` is a top-level metadata block built directly in `build_metadata()` / `build_metadata_from_arrangement()`, not nested under `pipeline.sources`.\n+- `_tool_fingerprints()` is a hard-coded allowlist in `tools/cut.py:503-517`.\n+\n+### C. `pipeline.py` mechanics\n+\n+#### Step order\n+\n+- `STEP_ORDER`:\n+  - `transcribe`\n+  - `scenes`\n+  - `shots`\n+  - `triage`\n+  - `scene_describe`\n+  - `quote_scout`\n+  - `pool_build`\n+  - `arrange`\n+  - `cut`\n+  - `refine`\n+  - `render`\n+  - `validate`\n+\n+#### Sentinel tuples\n+\n+- `PER_SOURCE_SENTINELS` is exactly:\n+  - `(\"transcript.json\", \"scenes.json\", \"shots.json\", \"scene_triage.json\", \"scene_descriptions.json\", \"quote_candidates.json\", \"pool.json\")`\n+- `PER_BRIEF_SENTINELS` is exactly:\n+  - `(\"arrangement.json\", \"hype.timeline.json\", \"hype.assets.json\", \"hype.metadata.json\", \"refine.json\", \"hype.mp4\", \"validation.json\")`\n+- Sentinel convention is artifact filenames, not `.done` files.\n+\n+#### `build_pool_steps()`\n+\n+- Per-source steps writing under `args.out`:\n+  - `transcribe`\n+  - `scenes`\n+  - `shots`\n+  - `triage`\n+  - `scene_describe`\n+  - `quote_scout`\n+  - `pool_build`\n+- Per-brief steps writing under `args.brief_out`:\n+  - `arrange`\n+  - `cut`\n+  - `refine`\n+  - `render`\n+  - `validate`\n+- `refine` is defined as a normal step in `build_pool_steps()`, but `pool_main()` only runs it when `args.render` is true.\n+\n+#### `sentinel_paths()` / skip-resume behavior\n+\n+- `sentinel_paths(step, args)` returns `[step_output_root(step, args) / name for name in step.sentinels]`.\n+- `step_output_root()` routes per-source steps to `args.out` and per-brief steps to `args.brief_out`.\n+- `should_rerun()` behavior:\n+  - forced rerun if `--from` covers the step\n+  - otherwise skip when all sentinels exist\n+  - special case: `refine` reruns if any of `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, or `hype.metadata.json` is newer than `refine.json`\n+  - special case: `render` reruns if any of `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, `hype.metadata.json`, or `refine.json` is newer than `hype.mp4`\n+  - special case: `cut` reruns on partial prior output if some but not all sentinels exist\n+- There is no `--resume` flag. Resume behavior is implicit via existing sentinel files and `should_rerun()`.\n+\n+#### `--from` / `--skip` validation\n+\n+- `resolve_args()` validates `--skip` against `STEP_ORDER` (plus legacy `picks` only in legacy mode).\n+- `resolve_args()` validates `--from` against `STEP_ORDER` (plus legacy `picks` only in legacy mode).\n+- Invalid step names raise `usage_error(...)`.\n+- Additional guard: `--skip cut` is rejected when `--render` is set and the expected `hype.timeline.json` is missing.\n+\n+#### Log routing\n+\n+- `log_dir_for_step(step, args)` writes logs to `<step_output_root>/logs`.\n+- `run_step()` writes combined stdout/stderr to `<root>/logs/<step>.log`.\n+- `write_skip_log()` writes the skip reason to the same `<root>/logs/<step>.log`.\n+- Result:\n+  - per-source stages log under `args.out/logs`\n+  - per-brief stages log under `args.brief_out/logs`\n+\n+#### `--render` gating\n+\n+- `pool_main()` contains:\n+  - `if step.name in {\"refine\", \"render\"} and not args.render: continue`\n+  - `if step.name == \"validate\" and not args.render: write_skip_log(...); continue`\n+- Therefore `refine` runs only under `--render`, and `validate` is explicitly skipped with a log when `--render` is absent.\n+\n+### D. Live metadata / artifact directory\n+\n+- `tools/runs/ados_pool/briefs/brief/hype.metadata.json` has top-level keys:\n+  - `clips`\n+  - `generated_at`\n+  - `pipeline`\n+  - `sources`\n+  - `version`\n+- `sources` is top-level, not under `pipeline`.\n+- `sources.main` currently contains:\n+  - `scenes_ref: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/tools/runs/ados_pool/scenes.json`\n+  - `transcript_ref: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/tools/runs/ados_pool/transcript.json`\n+  - `shots_ref: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/tools/runs/ados_pool/shots.json`\n+- Those refs are absolute paths under the pool root `tools/runs/ados_pool/`, not inside the brief directory `tools/runs/ados_pool/briefs/brief/`.\n+\n+### E. Optional `speaker` field\n+\n+- `tools/pool_build.py:113-128` writes dialogue entries with `\"speaker\": candidate.get(\"speaker\")`.\n+- `tools/runs/ados_pool/pool.json` confirms `speaker` is optional in live data:\n+  - some dialogue entries have `\"speaker\": null`\n+  - others have non-null speaker names\n+- Sample current values include:\n+  - `pool_d_0001 -> null`\n+  - `pool_d_0003 -> \"Peter [REDACTED] (Pom)\"`\n+  - `pool_d_0004 -> \"Zeef (LTX/Lightricks)\"`\n+\n+### F. Overlay constants\n+\n+- `tools/cut.py:616` defines `_MIN_OVERLAY_COVERAGE_SEC = 2.5`\n+- `tools/cut.py:618` defines `_MAX_VISUAL_HOLD_RATIO = 0.2`\n+- They are consumed in arrangement compilation at `tools/cut.py:752-766`.\n+- Least-invasive access choice for later tasks: import the underscored names directly from `cut.py` rather than lifting them to new public constants.\n+\n+### G. Pytest baseline\n+\n+- Command run from workspace root `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/`:\n+  - `pytest tools/tests -q --tb=no > .megaplan/baseline.txt 2>&1`\n+- Result summary:\n+  - `6 failed, 84 passed, 12 warnings, 23 subtests passed in 16.19s`\n+- Full failing nodeids set:\n+  - `tools/tests/test_arrange.py::ArrangeTest::test_cli_fills_hash_envelope_from_file_bytes`\n+  - `tools/tests/test_arrange.py::ArrangeTest::test_payload_passes_validate_arrangement`\n+  - `tools/tests/test_audio_render.py::AudioRenderTest::test_rendered_mp4_contains_audio_stream`\n+  - `tools/tests/test_hype_cut_invariants.py::test_hype_cut_invariants_on_discovered_run_dirs`\n+  - `tools/tests/test_multitrack_cut.py::MultitrackCutTest::test_compile_arrangement_plan_rejects_short_overlay`\n+  - `tools/tests/test_schema_contract.py::SchemaContractTest::test_generator_byte_stability`\n+- The three known red nodeids are present:\n+  - `tools/tests/test_arrange.py::ArrangeTest::test_cli_fills_hash_envelope_from_file_bytes`\n+  - `tools/tests/test_arrange.py::ArrangeTest::test_payload_passes_validate_arrangement`\n+  - `tools/tests/test_hype_cut_invariants.py::test_hype_cut_invariants_on_discovered_run_dirs`\n+- Additional baseline-red nodeids currently present:\n+  - `tools/tests/test_audio_render.py::AudioRenderTest::test_rendered_mp4_contains_audio_stream`\n+  - `tools/tests/test_multitrack_cut.py::MultitrackCutTest::test_compile_arrangement_plan_rejects_short_overlay`\n+  - `tools/tests/test_schema_contract.py::SchemaContractTest::test_generator_byte_stability`\n*** End Patch\n"

Implement `load(run_dir: Path) -> EnrichedArrangement` that reads: (a) `arrangement.json` via `timeline.load_arrangement()` (read-only), (b) `hype.timeline.json` if present for clip geometry, (c) per-asset `quality_zones.json` via TOP-LEVEL `hype.metadata.json[\"sources\"][asset][\"quality_zones_ref\"]` \u2014 NOT `pipeline.sources`; return zones=[] if the ref is absent or the file is missing (no `<run_dir>/..` fallback), (d) transcript and scenes caches via their respective `sources[asset].*_ref` entries, (e) the pool at `runs/<pool>/pool.json`. Joins on asset-key (`entry['asset']`, default `\"main\"`). Implement `apply_auto_fixes(enriched, findings)` as a pure in-memory mutator \u2014 persistence flows through `timeline.save_arrangement()` in refine.py. Caller-supplied `--out` is AUTHORITATIVE (matches tools/pipeline.py:322-346 contract). Detect `audio_dead` zones via `ffmpeg -i <src> -af silencedetect=n=<db>:d=<dur> -f null -` (parse stderr `silence_start=`/`silence_end=`). Detect `video_dead` zones via `ffmpeg -vf blackdetect=d=<dur>:pic_th=<pic_th> -f null -` (parse `black_start`/`black_end`). Write to `--out` path: `{\"source_sha256\": str, \"asset_key\": \"main\", \"zones\": [{\"kind\": \"audio_dead\"|\"video_dead\", \"start\": float, \"end\": float}, ...]}`. Content-keyed cache on `source_sha256`: on hit, re-emit the cached payload without re-running ffmpeg. Expose internal `compute(source_path: Path) -> QualityZonesReport` for unit tests only.         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 8 still makes draft creation happen only through the 800ms autosave path, while the publish flow immediately calls `syncembeds(postid, ...)` and `publishpost(postid, ...)`. if an author types content and clicks publish before the first autosave has created the row, there is no guaranteed `postid` for the primary publish path, so the plan has a runtime hole in the initial create-and-publish flow. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the cover fetch path is internally inconsistent. step 8 stores the selected cover on `posts.cover_media_id`, but step 5 says `usepost` fetches the post row plus `post_media` and `post_assets` and then 'resolves cover from `post.cover_media_id`'; because the cover picker is separate from body embeds and the plan never says to also link the chosen cover into `post_media`, a cover that is not embedded in the body would need its own direct media fetch or it will not be renderable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the inline video plan does not fit the existing media pipeline. `mediauploader` only returns `file` objects, and the current upload flow writes a public supabase url into `cloudflare_thumbnail_url`; meanwhile every existing video surface (`src/pages/artdetail/index.tsx`, `src/pages/resourcedetail/index.tsx`, and `src/pages/resources/hlsplayer.tsx`) expects `cloudflare_playback_hls_url` for playable video, so uploaded videos would not render through `hlsplayer` as planned. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed rpc auth path is internally inconsistent. `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role`, but its body authorizes with `auth.uid()` and `public.is_admin()`. in the current repo, edge functions authenticate the bearer separately with `supabase.auth.getuser(accesstoken)` and then run db work as service role (`supabase/functions/cloudflare-stream-ingest/index.ts:43-72`), so this sql function would not automatically see the caller's `auth.uid()` unless the plan adds a second authenticated client or rewrites the guard. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the storage/versioning sequence still has a race. step 3 uploads files under `post-bundles/bundles/<post_id>/<next_version>/...` before calling `register_bundle_version(...)`, but step 1 also makes the rpc compute `version = max(version) + 1` itself. concurrent uploads can therefore diverge between storage prefix and db version, and any rpc failure after upload leaves orphaned files because storage writes happened before the transactional sql step. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared step 3.11 in `plan_v6.md` with the vendored `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/timelinerenderer.tsx:66-85`. the plan's scaled visual branch wraps a bare `<visualclip .../>` in `<absolutefill style={{transform: scale(...)}}>` but does not include a surrounding `<sequence from=... durationinframes=...>`. in the reference, that branch wraps `<visualclipsequence .../>`, which is where clip timing is applied, so implementing the plan literally would make scaled non-position-override clips mount from frame 0 for the full composition instead of honoring `clip.at` and `getclipdurationinframes(...)`. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed cache contract for the `cut` step is technically unsafe because it uses only `hype.timeline.json` as the sentinel even though `cut.py` writes `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, and `hype.metadata.json` as one unit (`tools/cut.py:763-769`). later planned steps consume the extra outputs as required inputs (`tools/render_remotion.py:62-99` requires `--assets`, and the bridge copies `hype.assets.json`), so a partial prior run or manual deletion of assets/metadata would still be treated as 'cached' and cause downstream breakage. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

data = _load_json(path)\n+    if not isinstance(data, dict) or not isinstance(data.get(\"zones\"), list):\n+        return []\n+    zones: list[QualityZone] = []\n+    for item in data[\"zones\"]:\n+ Do not write `sys.path.insert` speculatively \u2014 if sibling imports work without it (which they do when `tools/` is on sys.path because pytest/subprocess set CWD appropriately), just use `import enriched_arrangement`.\n\n3. **CLI translation (gate warning)**: The plan's Step 10 uses commands that are not literally runnable:\n   - `python tools/pipeline.py --resume \u2026` \u2192 the pipeline has NO `--resume` flag. Use `--from quality_zones` or re-run with existing sentinels intact (pipeline skips complete stages automatically).\n   - `refine` only runs when `--render` is set (`tools/pipeline.py:88,754`). To exercise refine, include `--render` OR invoke `python tools/refine.py` directly against the brief dir.\n   - `pytest test_enriched_arrangement.py ...` \u2192 prepend `tools/tests/` to each path: `pytest tools/tests/test_enriched_arrangement.py tools/tests/test_quality_zones.py tools/tests/test_inspect_cut.py tools/tests/test_refine.py`.\n\n4. **Metadata nesting (FLAG-002 resolution)**: `quality_zones_ref` goes at TOP-LEVEL `sources.main.quality_zones_ref` in `hype.metadata.json`, NOT `pipeline.sources.main`. Verify by reading `tools/runs/ados_pool/briefs/brief/hype.metadata.json` \u2014 `sources` is a top-level key alongside `pipeline`, `clips`, etc.\n\n5. **Artifact location**: `quality_zones.json` lives at `runs/<pool>/quality_zones.json` (the pool root \u2014 sibling of `scenes.json`), NOT at `runs/<pool>/briefs/<brief>/quality_zones.json`. Brief dirs are two levels below pool root. In `refine.py:_regen_args()`, read `sources.main.scenes_ref`'s PARENT directory and append `quality_zones.json`. No `<run_dir>/..` fallback.\n\n6. **Sentinel convention**: Use `quality_zones.json` (the artifact filename) in `PER_SOURCE_SENTINELS`, NOT `quality_zones.done`. Matches the existing `transcript.json`/`scenes.json`/`shots.json` pattern.\n\n7. **Schema invariant**: No changes to arrangement schema beyond what's already there \u2014 no `warnings[]` field. Reviewer output lives in `refine.json` and per-asset `quality_zones.json` only. `tools/timeline.py` and `tools/remotion/` must remain unchanged (verified by `git diff` being empty).\n\n8. **Overlay constants**: `_MIN_OVERLAY_COVERAGE_SEC` and `_MAX_VISUAL_HOLD_RATIO` live in `tools/cut.py:614-618,752-766`. Pick the less invasive option.\n\n9. **Speaker flow**: Read `pool_entry.speaker` directly (not pool-id prefix). Emit NOTHING when either adjacent dialogue clip has `speaker=None`. Unit test must cover the None case.\n\n10. **overlay_fit**: Flag-only (no auto_fix). Single code: `overlay_insufficient_coverage`. Do NOT add `overlay_same_source_as_audio` \u2014 pool_d_* and pool_v_* IDs are distinct by construction.\n\n11. **STRUCTURE view data source**: Zone shading comes from per-asset `quality_zones.json` via `EnrichedArrangement` (populated from `sources.main.quality_zones_ref`), NOT from `hype.timeline.json`. Zero new regressions.         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 8 still makes draft creation happen only through the 800ms autosave path, while the publish flow immediately calls `syncembeds(postid, ...)` and `publishpost(postid, ...)`. if an author types content and clicks publish before the first autosave has created the row, there is no guaranteed `postid` for the primary publish path, so the plan has a runtime hole in the initial create-and-publish flow. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the cover fetch path is internally inconsistent. step 8 stores the selected cover on `posts.cover_media_id`, but step 5 says `usepost` fetches the post row plus `post_media` and `post_assets` and then 'resolves cover from `post.cover_media_id`'; because the cover picker is separate from body embeds and the plan never says to also link the chosen cover into `post_media`, a cover that is not embedded in the body would need its own direct media fetch or it will not be renderable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the inline video plan does not fit the existing media pipeline. `mediauploader` only returns `file` objects, and the current upload flow writes a public supabase url into `cloudflare_thumbnail_url`; meanwhile every existing video surface (`src/pages/artdetail/index.tsx`, `src/pages/resourcedetail/index.tsx`, and `src/pages/resources/hlsplayer.tsx`) expects `cloudflare_playback_hls_url` for playable video, so uploaded videos would not render through `hlsplayer` as planned. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed rpc auth path is internally inconsistent. `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role`, but its body authorizes with `auth.uid()` and `public.is_admin()`. in the current repo, edge functions authenticate the bearer separately with `supabase.auth.getuser(accesstoken)` and then run db work as service role (`supabase/functions/cloudflare-stream-ingest/index.ts:43-72`), so this sql function would not automatically see the caller's `auth.uid()` unless the plan adds a second authenticated client or rewrites the guard. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the storage/versioning sequence still has a race. step 3 uploads files under `post-bundles/bundles/<post_id>/<next_version>/...` before calling `register_bundle_version(...)`, but step 1 also makes the rpc compute `version = max(version) + 1` itself. concurrent uploads can therefore diverge between storage prefix and db version, and any rpc failure after upload leaves orphaned files because storage writes happened before the transactional sql step. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared step 3.11 in `plan_v6.md` with the vendored `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/timelinerenderer.tsx:66-85`. the plan's scaled visual branch wraps a bare `<visualclip .../>` in `<absolutefill style={{transform: scale(...)}}>` but does not include a surrounding `<sequence from=... durationinframes=...>`. in the reference, that branch wraps `<visualclipsequence .../>`, which is where clip timing is applied, so implementing the plan literally would make scaled non-position-override clips mount from frame 0 for the full composition instead of honoring `clip.at` and `getclipdurationinframes(...)`. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed cache contract for the `cut` step is technically unsafe because it uses only `hype.timeline.json` as the sentinel even though `cut.py` writes `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, and `hype.metadata.json` as one unit (`tools/cut.py:763-769`). later planned steps consume the extra outputs as required inputs (`tools/render_remotion.py:62-99` requires `--assets`, and the bridge copies `hype.assets.json`), so a partial prior run or manual deletion of assets/metadata would still be treated as 'cached' and cause downstream breakage. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: flagged that `--print-sql` still defaults `--timeline-id` to `<name>` even though `public.timelines.id` is a uuid column, so the default emitted `update` is not runnable as written. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: flagged that byte-preserving `hype.assets.json` into `public.timelines` remains technically incomplete because reigh-app resolves asset `file` values as http urls or `timeline-assets` bucket keys, while `cut.py` writes local absolute paths. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] audio-playback: audio playback: the port plan applies sanitized playback rate in `visualclip.tsx` but omits `playbackrate` from the planned `audiotrack.tsx`, leaving per-clip speed only partially implemented. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bundle-registration-auth: bundle registration auth: `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role` but authorizes with `auth.uid()`/`public.is_admin()` internally, which does not match the repo's existing edge function pattern where service-role clients validate the bearer separately before doing db work. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bundle-versioning: bundle versioning: step 3 uploads extracted files into a `<next_version>` storage prefix before the database allocates a version, but step 1 also computes `version = max(version) + 1` inside the rpc; concurrent uploads can mismatch storage and db state, and failed rpcs leave orphaned bundle files. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] community-section: success criterion 'all header-offset js styling goes through headeroffsetpadding()' is broader than what step 3 actually does (two css-string exceptions remain). (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 8 still makes draft creation happen only through the 800ms autosave path, while the publish flow immediately calls `syncembeds(postid, ...)` and `publishpost(postid, ...)`. if an author types content and clicks publish before the first autosave has created the row, there is no guaranteed `postid` for the primary publish path, so the plan has a runtime hole in the initial create-and-publish flow. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the cover fetch path is internally inconsistent. step 8 stores the selected cover on `posts.cover_media_id`, but step 5 says `usepost` fetches the post row plus `post_media` and `post_assets` and then 'resolves cover from `post.cover_media_id`'; because the cover picker is separate from body embeds and the plan never says to also link the chosen cover into `post_media`, a cover that is not embedded in the body would need its own direct media fetch or it will not be renderable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the inline video plan does not fit the existing media pipeline. `mediauploader` only returns `file` objects, and the current upload flow writes a public supabase url into `cloudflare_thumbnail_url`; meanwhile every existing video surface (`src/pages/artdetail/index.tsx`, `src/pages/resourcedetail/index.tsx`, and `src/pages/resources/hlsplayer.tsx`) expects `cloudflare_playback_hls_url` for playable video, so uploaded videos would not render through `hlsplayer` as planned. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed rpc auth path is internally inconsistent. `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role`, but its body authorizes with `auth.uid()` and `public.is_admin()`. in the current repo, edge functions authenticate the bearer separately with `supabase.auth.getuser(accesstoken)` and then run db work as service role (`supabase/functions/cloudflare-stream-ingest/index.ts:43-72`), so this sql function would not automatically see the caller's `auth.uid()` unless the plan adds a second authenticated client or rewrites the guard. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the storage/versioning sequence still has a race. step 3 uploads files under `post-bundles/bundles/<post_id>/<next_version>/...` before calling `register_bundle_version(...)`, but step 1 also makes the rpc compute `version = max(version) + 1` itself. concurrent uploads can therefore diverge between storage prefix and db version, and any rpc failure after upload leaves orphaned files because storage writes happened before the transactional sql step. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared step 3.11 in `plan_v6.md` with the vendored `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/timelinerenderer.tsx:66-85`. the plan's scaled visual branch wraps a bare `<visualclip .../>` in `<absolutefill style={{transform: scale(...)}}>` but does not include a surrounding `<sequence from=... durationinframes=...>`. in the reference, that branch wraps `<visualclipsequence .../>`, which is where clip timing is applied, so implementing the plan literally would make scaled non-position-override clips mount from frame 0 for the full composition instead of honoring `clip.at` and `getclipdurationinframes(...)`. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed cache contract for the `cut` step is technically unsafe because it uses only `hype.timeline.json` as the sentinel even though `cut.py` writes `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, and `hype.metadata.json` as one unit (`tools/cut.py:763-769`). later planned steps consume the extra outputs as required inputs (`tools/render_remotion.py:62-99` requires `--assets`, and the bridge copies `hype.assets.json`), so a partial prior run or manual deletion of assets/metadata would still be treated as 'cached' and cause downstream breakage.         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 8 still makes draft creation happen only through the 800ms autosave path, while the publish flow immediately calls `syncembeds(postid, ...)` and `publishpost(postid, ...)`. if an author types content and clicks publish before the first autosave has created the row, there is no guaranteed `postid` for the primary publish path, so the plan has a runtime hole in the initial create-and-publish flow. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the cover fetch path is internally inconsistent. step 8 stores the selected cover on `posts.cover_media_id`, but step 5 says `usepost` fetches the post row plus `post_media` and `post_assets` and then 'resolves cover from `post.cover_media_id`'; because the cover picker is separate from body embeds and the plan never says to also link the chosen cover into `post_media`, a cover that is not embedded in the body would need its own direct media fetch or it will not be renderable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the inline video plan does not fit the existing media pipeline. `mediauploader` only returns `file` objects, and the current upload flow writes a public supabase url into `cloudflare_thumbnail_url`; meanwhile every existing video surface (`src/pages/artdetail/index.tsx`, `src/pages/resourcedetail/index.tsx`, and `src/pages/resources/hlsplayer.tsx`) expects `cloudflare_playback_hls_url` for playable video, so uploaded videos would not render through `hlsplayer` as planned. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed rpc auth path is internally inconsistent. `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role`, but its body authorizes with `auth.uid()` and `public.is_admin()`. in the current repo, edge functions authenticate the bearer separately with `supabase.auth.getuser(accesstoken)` and then run db work as service role (`supabase/functions/cloudflare-stream-ingest/index.ts:43-72`), so this sql function would not automatically see the caller's `auth.uid()` unless the plan adds a second authenticated client or rewrites the guard. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the storage/versioning sequence still has a race. step 3 uploads files under `post-bundles/bundles/<post_id>/<next_version>/...` before calling `register_bundle_version(...)`, but step 1 also makes the rpc compute `version = max(version) + 1` itself. concurrent uploads can therefore diverge between storage prefix and db version, and any rpc failure after upload leaves orphaned files because storage writes happened before the transactional sql step. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared step 3.11 in `plan_v6.md` with the vendored `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/timelinerenderer.tsx:66-85`. the plan's scaled visual branch wraps a bare `<visualclip .../>` in `<absolutefill style={{transform: scale(...)}}>` but does not include a surrounding `<sequence from=... durationinframes=...>`. in the reference, that branch wraps `<visualclipsequence .../>`, which is where clip timing is applied, so implementing the plan literally would make scaled non-position-override clips mount from frame 0 for the full composition instead of honoring `clip.at` and `getclipdurationinframes(...)`. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed cache contract for the `cut` step is technically unsafe because it uses only `hype.timeline.json` as the sentinel even though `cut.py` writes `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, and `hype.metadata.json` as one unit (`tools/cut.py:763-769`). later planned steps consume the extra outputs as required inputs (`tools/render_remotion.py:62-99` requires `--assets`, and the bridge copies `hype.assets.json`), so a partial prior run or manual deletion of assets/metadata would still be treated as 'cached' and cause downstream breakage. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: flagged that `--print-sql` still defaults `--timeline-id` to `<name>` even though `public.timelines.id` is a uuid column, so the default emitted `update` is not runnable as written. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: flagged that byte-preserving `hype.assets.json` into `public.timelines` remains technically incomplete because reigh-app resolves asset `file` values as http urls or `timeline-assets` bucket keys, while `cut.py` writes local absolute paths. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] audio-playback: audio playback: the port plan applies sanitized playback rate in `visualclip.tsx` but omits `playbackrate` from the planned `audiotrack.tsx`, leaving per-clip speed only partially implemented. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bundle-registration-auth: bundle registration auth: `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role` but authorizes with `auth.uid()`/`public.is_admin()` internally, which does not match the repo's existing edge function pattern where service-role clients validate the bearer separately before doing db work. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bundle-versioning: bundle versioning: step 3 uploads extracted files into a `<next_version>` storage prefix before the database allocates a version, but step 1 also computes `version = max(version) + 1` inside the rpc; concurrent uploads can mismatch storage and db state, and failed rpcs leave orphaned bundle files.         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 8 still makes draft creation happen only through the 800ms autosave path, while the publish flow immediately calls `syncembeds(postid, ...)` and `publishpost(postid, ...)`. if an author types content and clicks publish before the first autosave has created the row, there is no guaranteed `postid` for the primary publish path, so the plan has a runtime hole in the initial create-and-publish flow. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the cover fetch path is internally inconsistent. step 8 stores the selected cover on `posts.cover_media_id`, but step 5 says `usepost` fetches the post row plus `post_media` and `post_assets` and then 'resolves cover from `post.cover_media_id`'; because the cover picker is separate from body embeds and the plan never says to also link the chosen cover into `post_media`, a cover that is not embedded in the body would need its own direct media fetch or it will not be renderable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the inline video plan does not fit the existing media pipeline. `mediauploader` only returns `file` objects, and the current upload flow writes a public supabase url into `cloudflare_thumbnail_url`; meanwhile every existing video surface (`src/pages/artdetail/index.tsx`, `src/pages/resourcedetail/index.tsx`, and `src/pages/resources/hlsplayer.tsx`) expects `cloudflare_playback_hls_url` for playable video, so uploaded videos would not render through `hlsplayer` as planned. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed rpc auth path is internally inconsistent. `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role`, but its body authorizes with `auth.uid()` and `public.is_admin()`. in the current repo, edge functions authenticate the bearer separately with `supabase.auth.getuser(accesstoken)` and then run db work as service role (`supabase/functions/cloudflare-stream-ingest/index.ts:43-72`), so this sql function would not automatically see the caller's `auth.uid()` unless the plan adds a second authenticated client or rewrites the guard. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the storage/versioning sequence still has a race. step 3 uploads files under `post-bundles/bundles/<post_id>/<next_version>/...` before calling `register_bundle_version(...)`, but step 1 also makes the rpc compute `version = max(version) + 1` itself. concurrent uploads can therefore diverge between storage prefix and db version, and any rpc failure after upload leaves orphaned files because storage writes happened before the transactional sql step. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared step 3.11 in `plan_v6.md` with the vendored `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/timelinerenderer.tsx:66-85`. the plan's scaled visual branch wraps a bare `<visualclip .../>` in `<absolutefill style={{transform: scale(...)}}>` but does not include a surrounding `<sequence from=... durationinframes=...>`. in the reference, that branch wraps `<visualclipsequence .../>`, which is where clip timing is applied, so implementing the plan literally would make scaled non-position-override clips mount from frame 0 for the full composition instead of honoring `clip.at` and `getclipdurationinframes(...)`. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed cache contract for the `cut` step is technically unsafe because it uses only `hype.timeline.json` as the sentinel even though `cut.py` writes `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, and `hype.metadata.json` as one unit (`tools/cut.py:763-769`). later planned steps consume the extra outputs as required inputs (`tools/render_remotion.py:62-99` requires `--assets`, and the bridge copies `hype.assets.json`), so a partial prior run or manual deletion of assets/metadata would still be treated as 'cached' and cause downstream breakage. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: flagged that `--print-sql` still defaults `--timeline-id` to `<name>` even though `public.timelines.id` is a uuid column, so the default emitted `update` is not runnable as written.         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 8 still makes draft creation happen only through the 800ms autosave path, while the publish flow immediately calls `syncembeds(postid, ...)` and `publishpost(postid, ...)`. if an author types content and clicks publish before the first autosave has created the row, there is no guaranteed `postid` for the primary publish path, so the plan has a runtime hole in the initial create-and-publish flow. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the cover fetch path is internally inconsistent. step 8 stores the selected cover on `posts.cover_media_id`, but step 5 says `usepost` fetches the post row plus `post_media` and `post_assets` and then 'resolves cover from `post.cover_media_id`'; because the cover picker is separate from body embeds and the plan never says to also link the chosen cover into `post_media`, a cover that is not embedded in the body would need its own direct media fetch or it will not be renderable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
